295: static inline uint32_t get_fraction_part(float num)
296: {
297: 	float result = num;
299: 	if (result < 0.0f)
302: 	// Remove the integer part from the value
303: 	result -= (float)get_int_part(result);
305: 	// Multiply the fractional part to convert it to an
306: 	// integer according to the PRIfloat macro (%06)
307: 	result *= 1000000.0f;
309: 	if (result < 0.0f)
311: 	result += 0.0000005f;
315: 	result += 0.0000005f;
318: 	return (uint32_t)result;
320: 	result -= (float)get_int_part(result);
323: 	result *= 1000000.0f;
326: 	return (uint32_t)result;
